# Chapter CCXXX

> An Act to establish certain Post-Roads.

*Be it enacted by the Senate and House of Representatives of the United States of America in Congress assembled,*

That the following be established as Post-Roads in lieu of those heretofore established, viz:

## MAINE.

From Alexandria to Wesley, through the town of Crawford.
